# Skills · P · part 76

822 entries, pitch-design to pixel-cycle. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pitch-design**: Design system skill for pitch. Activate when building UI components, pages, or any visual elements. Provides exact color tokens, typography scale, spacing grid, component patterns, and craft rules. Read references/DESIGN.md before writing any CSS or JSX. Includes ultra-mode visual journey: read references/ANIMATIONS.md, references/LAYOUT.md, references/COMPONENTS.md, and references/INTERACTIONS.md for full motion and layout details.
